### [1] [Diffeomorphism Invariance of the Favre-Filtered Compressible Navier--Stokes System under Spacetime Dilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11566)
*Yuanya Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明Favre滤波可压缩Navier–Stokes方程（含亚格子闭合项）在不同尺度坐标$(Lt,Lx_1,Lx_2,Lx_3)$下的系统，可通过时空流形上的光滑微分同胚从标准坐标系统得到，且解空间对所有$L>0$典范同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究尺度变换（坐标伸缩）下Favre滤波可压缩Navier–Stokes方程的结构保持性问题，明确参数化系统族之间的对称性与解空间等价关系。

Method: 首先自洽地处理无穷维流形与光滑场构型的Fréchet流形；定义单参数伸缩群$\{\varphi_L\}_{L>0}$并验证其为微分同胚，计算其切与余切作用；证明构型丛截面上的拉回作用与全导数算子交换，从而说明$\varphi_L$的一阶射流延拓将方程子流形映到自身；最后对方程（连续性、动量、能量）逐项验证。

Result: 每个残差乘以共同非零因子$L^{-1}$（或在变换坐标下恒为零），解集保持不变；参数化系统族满足微分同胚变换表达式。

Conclusion: 参数$L>0$的系统族满足微分同胚变换表达式，所有$L$对应的解空间典范同构。

Abstract: We prove that the system of Favre-filtered compressible Navier--Stokes equations with subgrid-scale closure terms, written in the coordinates $(Lt,Lx_1,Lx_2,Lx_3)$ for an arbitrary parameter $L>0$, is obtained from the system written in standard coordinates $(t,x_1,x_2,x_3)$ by the action of a smooth diffeomorphism of the underlying spacetime manifold. The proof begins with a self-contained treatment of infinite-dimensional manifolds and the Fréchet manifold of smooth field configurations. We then define the one-parameter dilation group $\{φ_L\}_{L>0}$, verify that each $φ_L$ is a diffeomorphism, and compute its tangent and cotangent actions. The induced pullback action on sections of the configuration bundle is shown to commute with the total derivative operator, which implies that the first-jet prolongation of $φ_L$ maps the equation submanifold of the system onto itself. Equation-by-equation verification---continuity, momentum, and energy---confirms that every residual is multiplied by the common nonzero factor $L^{-1}$ (or, equivalently, is identically zero in the transformed coordinates), so that the solution set is preserved. We conclude that the family of systems parameterised by $L>0$ satisfies the diffeomorphism transformation expression and that solution spaces are canonically isomorphic for all $L$.

</details>


### [2] [A sharp lower bound for the nodal volume of harmonic functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11444)
*Zhehui Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明了n≥3时非零实值调和函数零点集的(n-1)维Hausdorff测度下界与其倍增指标线性相关，即H^{n-1}({u=0}∩B_2)≥C_n·N，且线性依赖最优，证实了调和函数节点体积的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究调和函数零点集（节点集）的体积下界与函数复杂度（倍增指标）之间的关系，验证一个关于调和函数节点体积的猜想。

Method: 设u为B_4⊂R^n (n≥3)上的非零实值调和函数且u(0)=0，使用倍增指标N=log_2(sup_{B_1}|u|/sup_{B_{1/2}}|u|)刻画函数增长，通过几何测度论与调和分析技术估计零点集的(n-1)维Hausdorff测度。

Result: 证明H^{n-1}({u=0}∩B_2) ≥ C_n·N，其中C_n仅依赖n；并证明对N的线性依赖是最优的。

Conclusion: 该结果证实了调和函数节点体积的猜想，建立了零点集测度与倍增指标之间的线性下界估计。

Abstract: Let $u$ be a non-zero real-valued harmonic function in $B_4\subset\mathbb{R}^n$ with $n\geq3$ and $u(0)=0$. We prove that $$\mathcal{H}^{n-1}\bigl(\{u(x)=0\}\cap B_2\bigr)\ge C_n\mathcal{N},$$ where $C_n$ is a positive constant depending only on $n$, and $\mathcal{N}$ is the doubling index defined by $$\mathcal{N}=\log_2\frac{\sup_{B_1}|u|}{\sup_{B_{\frac{1}{2}}}|u|}.$$ The linear dependence on $\mathcal{N}$ is optimal. This estimate confirms a folklore conjecture on the nodal volume of harmonic functions.

</details>


### [3] [Numerical Study of Reported Multiple Solutions to a Cauchy Problem for the 2d Incompressible Euler Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11694)
*Allen Tesdall,Richard Sanders*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过精心设计的数值程序，研究Bressan-Shen（2021）与Bressan-Murray（2020）提出的二维不可压缩Euler方程Cauchy问题非唯一弱解猜想。针对初值在原点无界、问题定义于全平面、需保持基本对称性以及双参数可能临界关联等数值挑战，采用嵌套网格策略实现高阶、稳健、快速的离散格式，在大量参数组合中仅发现一组可能支持该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bressan与Shen（2021）及Bressan与Murray（2020）提出了一个含两个关键参数的二维不可压缩Euler方程Cauchy问题实例，猜想对两个特定近似初值的极限过程会产生两个不同的弱解。该猜想若成立，将揭示Euler方程弱解的非唯一性。然而该问题缺乏严格的解析证明，作者希望通过数值证据来检验该猜想是否成立、以及在何种条件下成立。额外的动机在于该问题本身具有多重数值困难：初值在原点邻域无界、定义域为全平面、须捕捉真解的多重对称性、且两个参数可能临界关联，这些都要求发展专门的数值方法。

Method: 采用数值实验方法。针对四个主要挑战（初值原点无界、全平面问题、需保持核心对称性、参数可能临界关联），作者论证了捕捉精确自由空间问题核心对称性在数学上需要极大的计算域，因此实施嵌套网格策略以兼顾适用性与效率，该方法可在解精度最关键区域实现极高网格加密。在嵌套网格框架内设计了高阶、稳健且快速的底层离散格式。随后遍历大量允许的参数对进行数值模拟。

Result: 在遍历的广泛参数对集合中，仅有唯一一组参数可能满足Bressan-Shen所提出的非唯一弱解猜想，其余参数组合均不支持该断言。

Conclusion: 数值证据表明Bressan-Shen（2021）与Bressan-Murray（2020）关于Euler方程非唯一弱解的猜想仅在极其狭窄的参数范围内（唯一一组参数）可能成立，说明该猜想的有效性受到参数临界关联的严格限制。研究同时验证了嵌套网格策略结合高阶离散格式是处理此类全平面、无界初值、需保持对称性的Euler问题的高效数值手段。

Abstract: Bressan and Shen (2021) jointly with Bressan and Murray (2020) proposed a simple example of a Cauchy problem on $\mathbb{R}^2$, characterized by two key parameters, intended to yield nonunique weak solutions of the incompressible Euler equations. They suggest that a limiting process applied to two specific approximations of their example initial data yields two distinct solutions. Here we provide evidence coming from a carefully designed numerical procedure to support if, or when, this intriguing claim is valid. Some of the numerical challenges encountered in the present study are: (i) the proposed initial data is unbounded in a neighborhood of the origin, (ii) the proposed problem is posed on all of $\mathbb{R}^2$, (iii) several essential symmetries present in the true solution must be captured, (iv) the proposed example depends on two parameters which may be critically linked. We demonstrate that an extremely large computational domain is mathematically necessary to capture the core symmetries of the exact free-space problem. Accordingly we implement a nested-grid strategy specifically designed for its particular suitability and efficacy for the problem at hand. Our strategy also allows us to achieve extreme grid refinement in the region where solution accuracy is most critical. Within our nested-grid framework, we design an underlying discretization scheme that is high-order, robust, and fast.
  We consider a broad collection of allowable parameter pairs, but among all of them we find only one that may satisfy the suggested claim.

</details>

### [4] [Weighted Empirical Risk Minimization for Machine Learning under Long-Range Dependence: Exact Pathwise Rates and Learning-Error Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10767)
*Elina Moldavskaya*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为长程依赖数据下、正则加权经验风险最小化训练的平滑参数模型建立了精确的几乎必然学习理论，给出几乎必然Bahadur表示、参数极限上确界律及轨迹簇集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典学习理论多建立在独立同分布或弱相依假设上，而实际时间序列常呈现长记忆。作者希望在长程依赖数据与正则加权经验风险最小化框架下，刻画学习过程的精确几乎必然渐近行为，弥补长记忆情形下学习理论的空白。

Method: 假设训练观测来自平稳高斯序列的固定有限窗口，样本权重正则变化。若总体最优处的损失梯度具有Wiener混沌秩m且低频系数非零，则在长记忆内部区域，有限滞后得分在对数迭代尺度上约化为单一加权Hermite混沌。基于该约化，推导几乎必然Bahadur表示、学习参数的精确上极限律，并在m≥2时给出完整学习轨迹的泛函簇集。进一步在秩一情形对可行幂权指数做全局优化。

Result: 得到几乎必然Bahadur表示与精确limsup律；m≥2时获得学习轨迹的泛函簇集。多项式学习指数由记忆参数与混沌秩决定，且在可行幂加权下不变；而尖锐的逐路径常数与簇几何依赖权重。秩一情形下，对可行幂指数的全局优化表明每个最优解均为正。时间序列预测与分类示例验证了结论。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了长程依赖下加权经验风险最小化的精确几乎必然学习理论，揭示了学习速率仅由记忆参数与混沌秩决定、而路径常数与簇几何受权重影响的分离现象，并通过预测与分类示例说明其适用性。

Abstract: We develop an exact almost-sure learning theory for smooth parametric models trained by regularly weighted empirical risk minimization on long-range dependent data. The training observations are generated from a fixed finite window of a stationary Gaussian sequence, and the sample weights are regularly varying. If the loss gradient at the population minimizer has Wiener-chaos rank $m$ and a nonzero low-frequency coefficient, then, in the long-memory interior regime, the finite-lag score reduces on the iterated-logarithm scale to a single weighted Hermite chaos. This yields an almost-sure Bahadur representation, an exact limsup law for the learned parameter, and, for $m\ge2$, the functional cluster set of the complete learning trajectory. The polynomial learning exponent is determined by the memory parameter and the chaos rank and is invariant under the admissible power weighting, whereas the sharp pathwise constant and cluster geometry depend on the weights. In the rank-one case, global optimization over the admissible power exponents shows that every optimizer is positive. Time-series prediction and classification examples illustrate the results.

</details>


### [5] [A Hilbert-Valued Functional Decomposition Framework for Explaining Time-Dependent Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11295)
*Sophie Hanna Langbein,Niklas Koenen,Marvin N. Wright,Julia Herbinger*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个面向时间依赖输出的基于特征解释的统一框架，通过将函数分解推广到Hilbert值预测函数并引入核化输出表示，实现多时间粒度的特征归因解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于特征的模型解释方法主要针对标量输出设计。但在许多应用中，输出是函数型或多变量的（如需求预测中的时间依赖轨迹），现有方法只能对每个输出位置独立解释，忽略了输出分量之间的依赖关系。因此需要一个能处理时间依赖输出、并考虑时间依赖性的统一解释框架。

Method: 作者将函数分解推广到Hilbert值预测函数，并将已有的基于特征的解释框架扩展至该设定；提出基于核的输出表示，使解释具备时间依赖性感知能力，支持多个时间粒度：特定时间、时间分辨、时间聚合；该框架提供统一视角，现有方法可作为其特例。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界数据上验证了框架，包括日内金融市场波动率预测和能源需求预测。

Conclusion: 该统一框架解决了时间依赖输出下特征解释忽略输出分量依赖的问题，通过核化输出表示支持多粒度时间解释，并在金融与能源等实际任务中验证了有效性，同时统一了既有方法。

Abstract: Feature-based explanations quantify features' influence on model predictions, but are primarily designed for scalar outputs. In many applications, however, outputs are functional or multivariate, such as time-dependent trajectories in demand forecasting. Consequently, existing approaches typically explain each output location independently, ignoring dependencies across the output components. We address this limitation by developing a unified framework for feature-based explanations of time-dependent outputs. Specifically, we generalize functional decomposition to Hilbert-valued prediction functions and extend an existing feature-based explanation framework to this setting. Our framework introduces kernel-based output representations that enable time-dependency-aware explanations at multiple levels of temporal granularity, including time-specific, time-resolved, and time-aggregated, while providing a unified view in which existing methods arise as special cases. We validate our framework on synthetic and real-world data, including intraday financial market volatility prediction and energy demand forecasting.

</details>


### [6] [Risk-Averse Decision Making with Multi-Level Reliability Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11524)
*Amirmohammad Farzaneh,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将"在不同目标中断水平下提供性能证书"的设计问题建模为对多个性能证书加权平均的最大化问题，证明其等价于在嵌套预测集上的优化，并给出可按输入值解耦的对偶形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线广播等工程应用需要在多个目标中断水平（outage level）上提供性能证书，而真实系统状态存在不确定性；已有工作（conformal prediction、单级风险规避决策）仅处理单一可靠性等级，无法刻画多级证书之间的权衡。

Method: 1) 将问题表述为在系统状态不确定情形下最大化多个性能证书的加权平均；2) 证明其等价于对嵌套预测集（nested prediction sets）的优化，从而与conformal prediction框架建立联系，并推广了单级风险规避决策的已有结果；3) 推导对偶形式，使优化在不同输入值之间解耦，便于求解。

Result: 在基于分集的无线传输系统上进行数值实验，量化了用单一共享策略满足多级证书所需付出的代价，并刻画了多个可靠性水平之间的Pareto权衡曲线。

Conclusion: 多级性能证书的联合优化可归约为嵌套预测集优化问题，且具备可解耦的对偶结构；单一共享策略在多个可靠性等级间存在不可避免的性能折衷，其Pareto前沿可由所提方法显式刻画。

Abstract: Many applications in engineering, including wireless broadcasting, require designs that provide performance certificates at different target outage levels. This paper studies the problem of maximizing the weighted average of such certificates in the presence of uncertainty about the true system state. The problem is shown to be equivalent to an optimization over nested prediction sets, connecting to the literature on conformal prediction and extending prior art on single-level risk-averse decision making. Furthermore, we derive a dual formulation that decouples optimization across input values. Numerical experiments on a diversity-based wireless transmission system illustrate the cost of enforcing multi-level certificates with a single shared policy and trace the Pareto trade-off between multiple reliability levels.

</details>


### [7] [A distribution-free certification framework for trustworthy crash-severity prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11592)
*Amir Rafe,Subasish Das*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一个与基础模型无关的认证层，用分布无关保证量化碰撞严重度（KABCO序数、报告噪声、跨辖区年份迁移）预测的有限样本意义，并给出内在信息量下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞严重度模型广泛用于筛查、调度和点位优先级，但缺乏有限样本的预测含义说明；现有通用保证在此失效，原因包括KABCO结果的序数性、记录标签与医学严重度仅约半数一致且误差有结构性、以及部署跨越标定未覆盖的辖区与年份。

Method: 构建一个包裹任意严重度模型且无需改动的认证层：连续序数集合（"B或更差"）；任意预设分区的逐类有效性及oracle效率刻画；通过声明报告带将覆盖率迁移到未观测真实严重度并给出最坏情况锐度；部署偏移下的单边证书；严重度加权风险控制；保证与可归因松弛预算可组合。同时用同一分析刻画认证可达上限：认证集合的信息量由真实分布的一个泛函支配，任何基础模型无法规避且无法分布无关地下界；在由记录链接数据识别出声明误报通道时，该下界可计算且非平凡。

Result: 在德州520万条记录、七个基础模型、跨越四十年的数据上，该层附加相同的有效性和认证；在易受伤害道路使用者上给出模型无关的集合宽度下界，无基础模型可突破，并将其与保持有界但分布无关不可识别的剩余部分分离。框架以开源包发布，含定理级测试。

Conclusion: 认证层为碰撞严重度模型提供分布无关的有限样本保证，并明确认证能力的极限，兼具实践部署价值与理论边界刻画。

Abstract: Crash-severity models inform screening, dispatch and site prioritization, yet are deployed without a finite-sample statement of what one prediction means. Off-the-shelf guarantees fail here, because the features that make crash severity distinctive defeat them: the KABCO outcome is ordinal, the recorded label is a field assessment agreeing with medical severity about half the time, erring in a structured way, and deployment crosses jurisdictions and years calibration never saw. We develop a certification layer that wraps any severity model unmodified, with distribution-free guarantees using this structure: contiguous ordinal sets that read as "B or worse"; per-class validity for any pre-declared partition, with an oracle efficiency characterization; transfer of coverage to unobserved true severity through a declared reporting band, with a worst-case sharpness result; a one-sided certificate under deployment shift; and severity-weighted risk control. The guarantees compose with an attributable slack budget. The same analysis bounds what certification can achieve. A certified set's informativeness is governed by a functional of the true law that no base model can evade and that cannot be lower-bounded distribution-free; given a declared misreporting channel identified from record-linkage data, a nonvacuous lower bound on that floor becomes computable. On 5.2 million Texas records across seven base models spanning four decades, the layer attaches identical validity and certifies, on the vulnerable road users, a model-independent floor on set width that no base model beats, separating it from a remainder that stays bounded but distribution-free unidentifiable. The framework is released as an open-source package with theorem-level tests.

</details>


### [8] [Near-Optimal Reinforcement Learning with Multi-Step Transition Lookahead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11807)
*Corentin Pla,Hugo Richard,Marc Abeille,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究带转移前瞻的强化学习：证明对任意固定有理折扣因子，精确规划仍为NP难；同时给出随机多项式时间近似方案，并扩展到未知转移与随机奖励，达到与经典表格折扣RL（仅差对数因子）匹配的累积遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转移前瞻能显著提升可达性能，但多步前瞻的最优规划已知是NP难的，然而该困难性结果依赖折扣因子趋近于1。因此遗留两个问题：对任意折扣因子问题是否仍然困难；以及是否仍能高效地进行近最优规划。

Method: 先证明对每个固定有理折扣因子γ∈(0,1)，精确规划保持NP难；再提出对每个固定前瞻深度均成立的随机多项式时间近似方案（近似算法/方案）；随后利用乐观主义与方差自适应置信界，将方法扩展到未知转移和随机奖励的情形。

Result: 解决了上述两个开放问题：精确规划对任意固定有理折扣因子仍为NP难；同时存在高效随机近似方案。所提学习算法的累积遗憾主项与经典表格折扣RL相同（至多差对数因子）。

Conclusion: 尽管带转移前瞻的精确规划是NP难的，但高效的近最优规划与学习仍然可行。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning (RL) with transition look-ahead, where the agent may observe which states would be visited upon playing any sequence of $\ell$ actions before deciding its course of action. Although look-ahead can substantially improve achievable performance, it is known that optimal planning with multi-step transition look-ahead is NP-hard, but this hardness was established using discount factors arbitrarily close to one. It was therefore unknown whether the problem remains hard for any discount factor, and whether near-optimal planning can nevertheless be performed efficiently. We resolve both questions. We then extend our approach to unknown transitions and stochastic rewards using optimism and variance-adaptive confidence bounds. The resulting algorithm achieves cumulative regret whose leading term matches classical tabular discounted RL up to logarithmic factors. Thus, although exact planning with transition look-ahead is NP-hard, efficient near-optimal planning and learning remain possible.

Moreover, Chronos-based CGM representations were more strongly correlated with observed postprandial glucose increments than representations from LSTM and CatBoost, even after those models incorporated additional dietary modalities, suggesting that pretrained temporal representations better preserve meal-induced excursion patterns. Consequently, we determine the complete Betti table for a general curve when $°L \geq 4g-3$ or when $°L \geq 3g-3$ and $L$ is general. We also explicitly compute the Boij--Söderberg coefficient of the section ring $R(C, L)$ governing asymptotic purity, and show eventual monotonicity of the remaining coefficients: they decrease for hyperelliptic curves and increase under a natural generic reducedness assumption on the relevant Brill--Noether loci.

</details>

*Ashveed Ashraf*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文通过"共识"（独立训练模型间的一致程度）量化无序系统物理描述的可复现性。在二维随机键Ising模型的师生框架下，学生从有限平衡构型中推断单一均匀有效耦合，其共识分布的方差可精确分解为有限采样贡献（下界为Fisher信息倒数）与淬火无序贡献，两者之和在赝临界温度附近取极小，且随无序增强而更尖锐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立学习者在同一无序系统的数据上训练时，有限且含噪的样本会导致它们对底层物理给出不同描述。作者希望量化这类描述的可复现性，即独立训练模型间的一致程度，并厘清一致性与推断忠实性之间的关系。

Method: 采用师生框架，在二维随机键Ising模型上，一组学生各自从单一淬火键实现所抽取的有限平衡构型中，推断一个均匀有效耦合。将学生与实现两维度上的推断耦合聚合，得到共识分布，用其宽度量化学习者间的一致程度。对共识分布的方差做精确分解。

Result: 方差精确分解为有限采样贡献与淬火无序贡献：采样项下界为Fisher信息倒数，并继承Fisher信息的临界峰；无序项在领头阶与温度无关，仅通过键无序的线性与三次响应的协方差，在无序强度四阶处获得临界极小。两者之和在临界区附近最小，故共识宽度在赝临界温度附近出现极小，并随无序增强而变尖锐。由于学生对异质晶格拟合均匀耦合，推断耦合还带有在无限数据极限下仍存在的误设定偏差，该偏差将均方误差的极小值推向共识极小值之上。

Conclusion: 独立学习者一致程度最高的温度并非其共同答案最忠实的温度：共识宽度极小值与均方误差极小值因误设定偏差而分离。这揭示了"可复现性"与"推断忠实性"是两个不同的量。

Abstract: When independent learners are trained on data from the same disordered system, finite and noisy samples can lead them to different descriptions of the underlying physics. We quantify the reproducibility of such descriptions through consensus, the degree of agreement among independently trained models. In a teacher-student framework on the two-dimensional random-bond Ising model, an ensemble of students each infers a single uniform effective coupling from a finite set of equilibrium configurations drawn from one quenched bond realization. Aggregating the inferred couplings across students and realizations yields the consensus distribution, whose width quantifies the agreement among learners. The variance of this distribution separates exactly into a finite-sampling contribution, bounded below by inverse of Fisher information, and a quenched-disorder contribution. Sum of these two variance is minimized near critical region, so the consensus width has a minimum near the pseudocritical temperature that sharpens with increasing disorder. The two minima arise from different mechanisms: the sampling term inherits the critical peak of the Fisher information, while the disorder term is temperature-independent at leading order and acquires its critical minimum only at fourth order in the disorder strength, through the covariance between the linear and cubic responses to the bond disorder. Because the students fit a uniform coupling to a heterogeneous lattice, the inferred coupling also carries a misspecification bias that survives in the infinite-data limit. This bias displaces the minimum of the mean-squared error above the consensus minimum, so the temperature at which independent learners agree most closely is not the temperature at which their shared answer is most faithful.

</details>


### [14] [Disordered ground states in one-dimensional exactly solvable fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11394)
*Igor Travěnec,Ladislav Šamaj*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究一维软排斥硬核粒子流体在等温-等压系综下的精确解，重点分析T→0基态的平均最近邻间距l0、对关联函数g0(x)及等温磁化率，发现凹势下存在"不可压缩"压力pi及相应的无序基态（非超均匀），且其预测能力可延伸至有限温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维体系因最近邻相互作用可获得精确热力学解，是理解有序-无序转变、超均匀性与基态结构关系的理想模型。本文旨在借助可调参数（ε、ν）的软排斥势，系统地考察势函数凹性如何决定零温基态构型、压力诱导的有序-无序转变及对关联函数的性质。

Method: 在等温-等压系综中研究直径a、通过有限程软排斥势φ(x)=ε[(a'-x)/(a'-a)]^(1/ν)（a≤x≤a'）成对相互作用的经典硬核粒子。当a'≤2a时，成对相互作用退化为最近邻相互作用，从而可精确求解热平衡。结合解析方法聚焦T→0基态，导出平均最近邻间距l0的状态方程及对关联函数g0(x)，并分ν≥1（凹）、ν>1（严格凹）、ν=1（线性斜坡）情形分别讨论。

Result: 当φ(x)为凹（ν≥1）时，存在不可压缩压力pi=ε/(a'-a)：基态在0<p<pi时为间距l0=a'的等距粒子链，在p>pi时为间距l0=a的等距粒子链。ν>1时，p=pi处基态无序，l0=[νa+(ν-1)a']/(2ν-1)，g0(x)为离散位置上加权Dirac δ函数的叠加；ν=1时，p=pi处基态无序，l0=(a+a')/2，连续g0(x)为Heaviside阶跃函数与x的多项式的叠加。T=0等温磁化率在ν≥1、p=pi时非零，故无序基态为非超均匀，类似有限温度的无序流体。无序基态的对关联函数虽仅在单一压力pi出现，但其预测能力可延伸至pi附近较宽压力范围的有限温度热力学态。

Conclusion: 软排斥势的凹性（ν≥1）是产生零温无序基态与非零等温磁化率的关键；此类无序基态仅在单一压力pi出现却具备非超均匀特征，其结构函数可预测pi附近有限温度下的热力学行为，揭示了一维精确可解模型中基态有序-无序转变与超均匀性的内在联系。

Abstract: One-dimensional fluids of classical hard-core particles of diameter $a$, interacting in pairs via a soft repulsive (monotonically decreasing) potential of finite range $\varphi(x)=\varepsilon \left[ (a'-x)/(a'-a)\right]^{1/ν}$ $(a\le x\le a')$ with real positive parameters $\varepsilon$ and $ν$, are studied in an isothermal-isobaric ensemble. If $a'\le 2a$, the pairwise interactions are reduced to nearest-neighbour interactions, which allows for an exact solution of the thermal equilibrium. We focus on the $T\to 0$ ground states, specifically on the equation of state for the mean distance between nearest neighbours $l_0$ and the pair correlation function $g_0(x)$. If $\varphi(x)$ is concave $(ν\ge 1)$, there exists an ``incompressibility'' pressure $p_i=\varepsilon/(a'-a)$ such that the ground state is an equidistant chain of particles with spacing $l_0=a'$ for $0<p<p_i$ and with spacing $l_0=a$ for $p>p_i$. If $ν>1$ (strict concavity), the ground state at $p=p_i$ is disordered with $l_0=\left[ νa +(ν-1)a'\right]/(2ν-1)$ and $g_0(x)$ being a superposition of weighted Dirac delta functions over discrete positions. If $ν=1$ (linear ramp), the ground state at $p=p_i$ is disordered with $l_0=(a+a')/2$ and the continuous $g_0(x)$ is a superposition of Heaviside step functions multiplied by polynomials in $x$. The isothermal susceptibility at $T=0$ is nonzero for concave $\varphi(x)$ ($ν\ge 1$) at $p=p_i$ and, therefore, the corresponding disordered ground states are non-hyperuniform, i.e., they resemble disordered fluids at nonzero temperatures. It turns out that pair correlation functions of disordered ground states, which occur only at a single pressure $p_i$, extend their predictive power to thermodynamic states at nonzero temperatures over a wider range of pressures around $p_i$.

</details>

*Tingfei Li,Jie Gu*

TL;DR: 该论文研究时间随机可积量子电路中的流涨落，利用可积性将退火涨落约化为少数"层组成"历史类之间的竞争，预测了主导历史类之间的一阶切换及尖点终止线，并分析了有限时间下的圆化效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间随机驱动通常破坏可积性，但可积电路中的算符对易性使得驱动历史可被精确压缩。作者希望理解在退火（历史平均）涨落中，时间随机性本身如何组织轨迹空间中的统计行为，即随机性是否可作为类似序参量的坐标。

Result: 退火大偏差收缩预测主导历史类之间存在一阶切换，并在参数空间中终止于一条由正则尖点（regular cusp）端点构成的线；有限时间分析表明该切换会被圆化（rounded），即尖锐切换在有限时间内平滑过渡。

Conclusion: 时间随机性可作为轨迹空间中涌现的、类序参量的坐标，主导流涨落的类切换。该工作为随机可积系统中的非平衡统计提供了可解析处理的框架，并连接了大偏差理论与可积TBA方法。

Abstract: We study current fluctuations in a temporally random integrable quantum circuit. Commutativity reduces every drive history exactly to its layer composition, turning annealed fluctuations into a competition between current gain and the large-deviation cost of rare compositions. For each fixed composition, homogeneous thermodynamic Bethe ansatz dressing supplemented by ballistic fluctuation theory yields the conditional current statistics. Their annealed large-deviation contraction predicts a first-order switch between two dominant history classes, terminating on a line of regular cusp endpoints. Finite-time analysis shows how the switch is rounded. Thus temporal randomness can act as an emergent order-parameter-like coordinate in trajectory space.

</details>


### [16] [Work as a function of protocol duration for the efficient erasure of an underdamped memory: isothermal to adiabatic transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11473)
*Nicolas Barros,Stephen Whitelam,Sergio Ciliberto,Ludovic Bellon*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 使用进化强化学习为欠阻尼双势阱悬臂梁设计高效时间依赖的擦除协议，发现擦除平均功随协议时长的标度在准静态等温区和绝热区呈现两种不同行为，且学习所得协议可优于受平衡边界条件约束的最优协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究1比特记忆（双势阱中运动的欠阻尼悬臂梁）擦除过程所需平均功如何随协议时长标度，并考察欠阻尼情形与过阻尼情形的差异，以理解记忆擦除的有限时间热力学代价及其下界。

Method: 采用进化强化学习来搜索高效的时间依赖擦除协议，针对处于双势阱势中的欠阻尼悬臂梁这一1比特记忆的实验实现，分析平均擦除功⟨W⟩随协议时长τ的标度关系，并通过一个思想实验（gedanken）最优协议推导下界。

Result: 发现两个区域：当τ≫tr（准静态等温区）时，⟨W⟩趋于Landauer界并叠加一个按1/τ标度的额外开销，与过阻尼情形相似；当τ<tr时，擦除变为绝热过程，⟨W⟩增长比等温情形更慢，其增长下界为1/τ。与过阻尼擦除的对比显示，学习得到的协议能够优于在平衡边界条件下最优的协议。

Conclusion: 欠阻尼1比特记忆的擦除功随协议时长呈现等温与绝热两种标度区制，绝热区增长更慢且下界为1/τ；进化强化学习可超越平衡边界条件下的最优协议，表明学习型协议在有限时间擦除中具有优势。

Abstract: We use evolutionary reinforcement learning to determine efficient time-dependent erasure protocols for an underdamped cantilever moving in a double-well potential, an experimental realization of a 1-bit memory. We investigate how the mean work $\langle W \rangle $ needed to erase a bit scales as a function of the protocol duration $τ$. We find two regimes, depending on how $τ$ compares to the relaxation time of the system $t_r$. For $τ\gg t_r$, the quasistatic isothermal regime, we recover Landauer's bound plus an overhead that scales as $1/τ$, similar to the overdamped case. By contrast, for $τ<t_r$ erasure becomes adiabatic and $\langle W \rangle$ grows more slowly than in the isothermal case. This growth is bounded from below as $1/τ$, which we derive using a gedanken optimal protocol. Finally, comparison with overdamped erasure shows that learned protocols can outperform protocols that are optimal subject to equilibrium boundary conditions.

</details>

### [17] [EVTradeMatch: A Mobility-Aware Multi-Objective Matching Framework for EV--EV Energy Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10551)
*Md. Mahfujur Rahman,Alistair Barros,Raja Jurdak,Darshika Koggalahewa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出EVTradeMatch，一个预测引导的多目标优化框架，用于移动性感知的电动汽车（EV）之间点对点能量交易，通过NSGA-II近似Pareto最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限充电基础设施下，EV间点对点能量交易可提升充电灵活性，但有效交易需在行程特定条件下协调提供者与消费者匹配，现有方法（基于邻近或拍卖）难以同时兼顾多目标与移动性因素。

Method: 基于前期EVNextTrade学习排序模型，定义充电节点适宜性为反映提供者-消费者对在候选节点分配恰当程度的预测得分，作为引导信号和显式优化目标而非硬性选择规则；将EV-EV匹配建模为多目标混合整数线性规划，在时空、一对一匹配及节点容量约束下最大化匹配覆盖率、转移能量和节点适宜性，最小化移动成本；设计定制NSGA-II近似Pareto有效解。

Result: 相比基于邻近和拍卖的先进方法，转移能量提升74.2–82.8%，节点适宜性提升8.3–84.4%，匹配覆盖率提升3.74–25.07个百分点；均衡NSGA-II解达到53.07±0.76%匹配覆盖率和1701.94±17.03 kWh转移能量，移动成本作为对旅行最小化方法的显式权衡而更高；Pareto前沿分析显示框架支持按运营优先级灵活选择高覆盖、高能量、低移动成本或高适宜性方案。

Conclusion: EVTradeMatch通过预测引导的多目标优化有效提升EV-EV能量交易的转移能量、节点适宜性和匹配覆盖率，Pareto前沿提供灵活方案选择，但以更高移动成本为代价。

Abstract: Peer-to-peer energy trading among electric vehicles (EVs) can improve charging flexibility under limited charging infrastructure, but effective EV--EV trading requires coordinated provider--consumer matching under journey-specific conditions. This paper proposes EVTradeMatch, a prediction-guided multi-objective optimization framework for mobility-aware EV--EV energy trading. Building on the EVNextTrade study, a prior learning-to-rank model for charging-node recommendation, we define charging-node suitability as a prediction-derived score reflecting the appropriateness of assigning a provider--consumer pair to a candidate charging node based on mobility, energy, and contextual trading features. This score is used as a guidance signal and as an explicit optimization objective rather than as a hard selection rule. The EV--EV matching problem is formulated as a multi-objective mixed-integer linear program that maximizes matching coverage, transferred energy, and charging-node suitability while minimizing mobility cost under spatial, temporal, one-to-one matching, and charging-node capacity constraints. To approximate Pareto-efficient solutions in wide-area dynamic settings, we develop a tailored non-dominated sorting genetic algorithm II (NSGA-II). Experimental results show that EVTradeMatch improves transferred energy by 74.2--82.8% and charging-node suitability by 8.3--84.4% compared with proximity- and auction-based state-of-the-art methods, while improving matching coverage by 3.74--25.07 percentage points. Balanced NSGA-II solutions achieve 53.07$\pm$0.76% matching coverage and transfer 1701.94$\pm$17.03 kWh, with higher mobility cost as an explicit trade-off against travel-minimizing methods. Pareto-front analysis shows that the framework supports flexible selection among high-coverage, high-energy, low-mobility-cost, and high-suitability solutions according to operational priorities.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究两类非线性系统的非自适应全局鲁棒扰动抑制问题（C1类：严格反馈形式、线性Hurwitz零动态并含强迫性非匹配加性扰动；C2类：最小相位不确定非线性系统、相对阶大于一、含匹配加性扰动），分别基于高增益范式与开环观测器提出控制架构，实现闭环输入-状态稳定性与全局一致有界性，并使吸引子可任意缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对两类非线性系统需在非自适应、输出反馈条件下实现全局鲁棒扰动抑制的问题。C1类系统中零动态状态不可测且存在非匹配加性扰动，C2类系统中扰动未知导致经典动态扩展方法不可实现，因此需要新的控制架构在保证稳定性的同时抑制扰动并降低高增益控制代价。

Method: 针对C1类，基于高增益范式设计控制架构，采用非线性工具证明闭环输入-状态稳定性及向可任意缩小吸引子的全局渐近收敛，并利用该稳定性嵌入基于滑模范式的全局一致有界控制单元以改善扰动抑制、降低高增益开销。针对C2类，先以开环观测器替代经典动态扩展以解决未知强迫扰动下的输出反馈问题，再将C1的结果推广至C2，得到输出反馈动态控制器。

Result: C1类系统在所提架构下实现闭环输入-状态稳定性（相对强迫扰动）及向可任意缩小吸引子的全局渐近收敛，并附加一致有界控制作用；C2类系统通过开环观测器与推广的控制器实现闭环全局一致有界性及向可任意缩小吸引子的渐近调节。滑模附加单元可潜在地降低所需的高增益控制开销。

Conclusion: 所提出的非自适应控制架构能有效解决C1与C2两类非线性系统的全局鲁棒扰动抑制问题：高增益范式结合滑模附加单元兼顾稳定性与扰动抑制效率，开环观测器替代动态扩展使C2类系统在未知扰动下仍可实现输出反馈控制，吸引子可任意缩小，具有良好的鲁棒性与工程可实现性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of non-adaptive global robust disturbance rejection for two distinct classes of nonlinear systems. The first class, denoted by C1, consists of nonlinear systems in strict-feedback form, with linear and Hurwitz zero-dynamics (whose states are unavailable for feedback), and enhanced - within this work - by forcing, unmatched, additive disturbances. Nonlinear tools are herein employed to demonstrate that the proposed control architecture - based on the high-gain paradigm - achieves closed-loop input-to-state stability with respect to the forcing disturbances, along with global asymptotic convergence towards an attractor which can be rendered as small as desired. Then, owing to the established input-to-state stability property, a uniformly bounded control action is additionally embedded within the control architecture. The additional unit, designed following the sliding-mode paradigm, is aimed at improving the disturbance rejection task, by potentially lowering the required high-gain control expenditure. The second class of systems, denoted by C2, is constituted by minimum-phase, uncertain, nonlinear systems with relative degree greater than one, featuring possibly unbounded, with possibly unbounded derivatives, output-dependent nonlinearities, with matched additive forcing disturbances. To solve the problem of output-feedback, non-adaptive, global robust disturbance rejection for systems within C2, first, an open-loop observer is employed in lieu of a classic dynamic extension adopted in earlier works, as the latter is no longer implementable due to the presence of unknown forcing disturbances. Subsequently, the results derived for C1 are then adapted to C2, to yield an output-feedback dynamic controller providing closed-loop global uniform boundedness, along with asymptotic regulation towards an attractor which can be rendered as small as desired.

</details>

Method: 以诺特定理第二定理为理论基础，建立描述被控对象的动力系统规范对称性与Pareto集结构之间的基本联系；将Pareto前沿视为系统及质量准则关于某对称群不变时对应的守恒律；提出"规范正则化"概念，用于剔除不影响质量准则的变量以约简解空间维度。

Result: 理论上证明了规范对称性与Pareto集结构之间的对应关系，并给出Pareto前沿的守恒律解释；规范正则化可在保持Pareto集不变的同时实现变量消去与降维。数值实验在两个冲突准则的线性二次调节器和标准ZDT1问题上给出了示例验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为多目标变分控制问题的Pareto集提供了一种基于对称性和守恒律的解析构造框架，规范正则化有效降低了解空间维度，为高维多目标优化提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: This paper presents an analytical approach to constructing the Pareto set in multiobjective variational control problems, based on Noether's second theorem. A fundamental connection is established between the gauge symmetries of the dynamical system describing the plant and the structure of the Pareto set. It is shown that the Pareto front can be interpreted as a conservation law arising from the invariance of the system and the quality criteria with respect to a symmetry group. A gauge regularization concept is proposed, which allows eliminating variables that do not affect the criteria without changing the Pareto set, thereby reducing the dimension of the solution space. Numerical examples are provided for the linear-quadratic regulator with two conflicting criteria and for the standard ZDT1 test problem.

</details>

*Seyyed Ahmad Edalatpanah*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出 PEJWAK——一种"轮廓呼应式自锚定"多准则决策聚合架构，各准则在聚合前先与由同一方案导出的内生参照几何耦合，从而在保留准则身份的同时使其贡献受方案轮廓调节。给出通用形式化框架与典型成员（加权算术自锚、保留指数等于准则权重、归一化平方根贡献系数），并建立一系列解析性质与确定性审计工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接加权聚合无法刻画准则贡献随方案轮廓（profile）变化的特性，而参数密集的非可加交互模型（如模糊积分类）又缺乏可审计性与可解释性。因此需要一个介于两者之间、既保留准则身份又可分解审计的聚合机制。

Method: 提出轮廓呼应的自锚定聚合架构：将每条准则与源自同一方案的内生参照几何耦合，再执行外层聚合。通用形式化将锚函数、锚定核、保留指数、贡献系数与外层聚合规则分离；典型成员采用加权算术自锚、保留指数取对应准则重要度、贡献系数由归一化平方根映射导出。并配套确定性 MCDA 审计流程，包括准则级完全分解、精确权重稳定区间与排序跃迁、贡献规则排序相位、集合依赖性诊断及基于置换的精确排序亲和度分析。

Result: 解析结果证明该架构具有连续性、单调性、对角校准性、内部性、全定义域联合连续性、精确边界行为、非可分性，在等权二元限制下不满足双对称性，并服从精确的对数保留律。典型 PEJWAK 无需 epsilon 正则化即可处理精确零值：只要其余有效得分仍支撑正锚，活跃零仅移除其自身准则贡献。

Conclusion: PEJWAK 提供了介于直接加权聚合与参数密集型非可加交互模型之间的可审计、轮廓条件化聚合机制，兼顾准则身份保留与可分解的决策审计能力。

Abstract: PEJWAK is introduced as a profile-echoing self-anchored aggregation architecture for multi-criteria decision analysis. Each criterion is coupled geometrically with an endogenous reference derived from the same alternative before final aggregation, so criterion identity is preserved while its expressed contribution is conditioned by the alternative's profile. A general formulation separates the anchor function, anchoring kernel, retention exponents, contribution coefficients, and outer aggregation rule. The canonical member uses a weighted-arithmetic self-anchor, sets each retention exponent equal to the corresponding criterion-importance value, and derives contribution coefficients through a normalized square-root map. Analytical results establish continuity, monotonicity, diagonal calibration, internality, full-domain joint continuity, exact boundary behavior, nonseparability, failure of bisymmetry in the equal-weight binary restriction, and an exact logarithmic retention law. A deterministic MCDA audit provides complete criterion-level decomposition, exact weight-stability intervals and rank transitions, contribution-rule rank phases, set-dependence diagnostics, and exact permutation-based rank-affinity analysis. Canonical PEJWAK handles exact zeros without epsilon regularization: an active zero removes only its own criterion contribution whenever the remaining active scores sustain a positive anchor. The architecture therefore supplies an auditable profile-conditioned aggregation mechanism between direct weighted aggregation and parameter-intensive nonadditive interaction models.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算子分裂方法（如前后向分裂、Douglas-Rachford分裂、ADMM）中的预条件子通常需要人工设定，缺乏系统性调节手段，且固定的预条件子难以适应优化过程中的动态需求，限制了收敛速度。

Method: 将前后向分裂、Douglas-Rachford分裂和ADMM统一解释为作用于其对应包络函数上的预条件梯度下降；在此基础上引入在线学习机制，在优化过程中自适应地学习并调节预条件子。

Result: 该框架获得了渐近更快的收敛速率，并在Lasso、ℓ1正则化逻辑回归和二次规划等重要优化问题上加速了求解；大量数值实验验证了理论结果并证明了方法的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 预条件子可被视为包络函数梯度下降中的可学习参数，通过在线学习自动调节，从而系统性提升分裂方法的收敛性能，为多种分裂算法提供了统一且实用的加速框架。

Abstract: This paper develops a principled framework for automatically tuning preconditioners in operator splitting methods, including forward-backward splitting, Douglas-Rachford splitting, and the alternating direction method of multipliers. We interpret these splitting methods as preconditioned gradient descent applied to their corresponding envelope functions and use online learning to adaptively learn the preconditioner during optimization. Our framework achieves asymptotically faster convergence rates and accelerates the solution of important optimization problems, including Lasso, $\ell_1$-regularized logistic regression, and quadratic programming. Extensive numerical experiments validate our theoretical results and demonstrate the practical effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [22] [A Simple Complexity Lower Bound for Solving $Ax=b$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10874)
*Ohad Shamir*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文给出一个简短直接的证明：在无维度限制下，将 $Ax=b$ 求解到相对误差 $\varepsilon$（$A$ 条件数为 $\kappa$），即使对随机算法，最坏情况也需要 $\Omega(\kappa\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 次矩阵-向量乘法，并通过简单归约得到强凸二次函数优化的经典 $\Omega(\sqrt{\kappa}\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Dereziński、Epperly 和 Meyer [2026]）给出了更一般且优雅的下界方法，但作者希望提供一个更简短、更直接的证明来恢复该设定下的下界结果。

Method: 作者采用简短直接的证明技巧，在 $A$ 条件数为 $\kappa$ 且维度不受限制的设定下，证明求解 $Ax=b$ 到相对误差 $\varepsilon$ 的随机算法最坏情况需要 $\Omega(\kappa\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 次矩阵-向量乘法；并借助一个直接的归约，将该结果推广到强凸二次函数优化问题。

Result: 证明了在最坏情况下，即使对于随机算法，求解 $Ax=b$ 到相对误差 $\varepsilon$ 需要 $\Omega(\kappa\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 次矩阵-向量乘法；通过直接归约，进一步得到强凸二次函数优化的 $\Omega(\sqrt{\kappa}\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 下界，且适用于随机算法。

Conclusion: 本文以简单直接的方式恢复了已有文献中关于该设定下的下界结果，并表明经典强凸二次优化下界可由该结果通过直接归约导出。

Abstract: In this note, we provide a short and direct proof that approximately solving $Ax=b$ to relative error $\varepsilon$, where $A$ has condition number $κ$ and unrestricted dimension, requires $Ω(κ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ matrix-vector multiplications in the worst case, even for randomized algorithms. This essentially recovers the lower bound of Dereziński, Epperly and Meyer [2026] for this setting, whose elegant and more general approach inspired us to seek a short direct proof. A straightforward reduction implies the classical $Ω(\sqrtκ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ lower bound for optimizing strongly convex quadratic functions, applicable to randomized algorithms.

</details>


### [23] [Optimal chemo-immunotherapy scheduling: A hybrid QPSO-SQP approach with Michaelis-Menten pharmacodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11213)
*Bereket Sitotaw Kidane,Md Samiul Haque Motayed,Shuo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将Michaelis-Menten饱和直接嵌入治疗通道，使联合化免治疗最优调度变为非控制仿射问题；在正暴露惩罚下，Hamiltonian具有显式三区间逐点最小化律（下界、上界或唯一内点），内点区间满足严格Legendre条件，从而产生连续调制的正则内极值解。采用QPSO-SQP混合算法求解非凸转写问题，共态重构验证了PMP结构与边界/内点区间切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合化免治疗的最优调度通常被建模为控制仿射最优控制问题，其解一般为边界选择的bang-bang型方案（除非出现奇异弧）。这种结构虽便于求解，却忽略了高剂量率下的药效饱和现象，导致模型与实际药理动力学不符。因此需要将饱和动力学纳入治疗通道以刻画更真实的剂量-效应关系。

Method: 将Michaelis-Menten饱和直接纳入治疗通道，使系统动力学不再关于控制仿射，Hamiltonian对每个控制呈非线性。在严格正暴露惩罚下推导出Hamiltonian的显式三区间逐点最小化律：每个输入取下界、上界或唯一内点极小值。针对由此产生的非凸转写问题，采用QPSO提供满足约束的warm start，再由SQP在配点网格上强制可行性与KKT最优性，并通过共态重构验证PMP结构。

Result: 内点区间上严格Legendre条件成立，连续调制的给药方案作为正则内极值出现，而非奇异弧或平滑伪迹。共态重构结果与PMP结构及预测的边界/内点区间切换一致，验证了所提三区间最小化律与混合求解框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 将Michaelis-Menten饱和嵌入治疗通道可突破传统控制仿射框架下bang-bang解的局限，生成具有严格Legendre条件的连续调制给药方案；QPSO-SQP混合算法结合共态重构能够有效求解并验证该类非凸最优控制问题。

Abstract: Optimal scheduling of combined chemo-immunotherapy is often formulated as a control-affine optimal control problem, which generically yields boundary-selected (bang-bang-type) protocols unless singular arcs occur. This structure, while convenient, neglects saturating pharmacodynamics at high dose rates. We incorporate Michaelis-Menten saturation directly into the therapy channels, making the dynamics non-control-affine and the Hamiltonian nonlinear in each control. With strictly positive exposure penalties, the Hamiltonian admits an explicit three-regime pointwise minimization law: each input is chosen at the lower bound, the upper bound, or as a unique interior minimizer. On interior intervals the strict Legendre condition holds, so continuously modulated dosing arises as a regular interior extremal rather than a singular-arc or smoothing artifact. The resulting transcription is nonconvex; we therefore use a hybrid Quantum Particle Swarm Optimization (QPSO)-Sequential Quadratic Programming (SQP) pipeline, where QPSO provides a constraint-aware warm start and SQP enforces feasibility and Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) optimality on a collocation grid. Costate reconstruction corroborates the Pontryagin Minimum Principle (PMP) structure and the predicted boundary/interior regime transitions.

</details>

Combining these two methods, we introduce the p-q quasi-norm Subspace Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (pqSEDMD). Abstract: Seasonal reservoir operation requires optimization methods that remain reliable under strongly periodic and uncertain inflow dynamics. We present InFlow, a verification-oriented framework combining periodic stochastic dual dynamic programming (SDDP), entropic robustness, control-space Bellman annealing, and an independently discretized Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) benchmark. The entropic transition operator is interpreted as a KL-penalized worst-case expectation and linked to fixed-radius KL ambiguity sets through Lagrange duality. We establish a uniform soft-Bellman approximation bound and prove the validity of Gibbs-tilted SDDP cuts. The benchmark relies on a normalized seasonal Cox-Ingersoll-Ross inflow process whose positivity condition fails during the driest weeks, requiring explicit treatment of the degenerate boundary. Independent verification shows strong agreement between HJB storage gradients and HiGHS storage-balance duals, with water-value correlations of 0.995 and 0.996 for the standard and exact-cut-only implementations. Refining the HJB grid from 61^2 to 81^2 states changes the annual mean water value by only 0.6% and the peak value by less than 0.1%. Out-of-sample simulations based on 3,000 independent trajectories show that a robustness level of gamma=5 increases nominal mean cost by 4.6% while reducing CVaR90 by 11.2%. Under a dry-season stress scenario with inflows reduced by 40%, the mean-cost difference becomes statistically insignificant whereas CVaR90 improves by 11.5%. A robustness sweep confirms monotonicity of the risk-adjusted value. Finally, a two-reservoir cascade reproduces grid-oracle marginal water values with correlation 0.999 and recovers the predicted upstream-to-downstream value ratio of two. InFlow is intended as a transparent methodological benchmark rather than a calibrated operational reservoir model.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the classical time-optimal elevator problem in the presence of higher-order state constraints. The main contribution is a constructive indirect solution framework based on a Pontryagin Maximum Principle specifically formulated for higher-order constrained systems. First, we derive specialized optimality conditions and analyze the resulting structure of extremal trajectories. Second, we show that the infinite-dimensional optimal control problem can be transformed into a finite-dimensional system of nonlinear algebraic equations involving switching times, boundary-contact times, and multiplier parameters. This provides a computationally efficient procedure for calculating candidate optimal trajectories using standard nonlinear equation solvers. Third, we characterize the geometry of boundary contacts and explain the emergence of contact-chattering phenomena in higher-order constrained systems. In particular, we show that normal extremals cannot evolve along the constraint boundary for a positive amount of time, although sequences of boundary contacts may accumulate indefinitely. The proposed approach is demonstrated on a fourth-order elevator model, and the computed solutions are independently verified using a direct IPOPT-based optimization method.

Abstract: We study asynchronous replanning in a two population linear quadratic mean field game in which the populations may begin from different beliefs and hence use different plans. Each population observes its own aggregate trajectory and a public record of implemented revisions, while its continuation best response depends on the opponent's current plan. We identify the information required for replanning as the aggregate state at the end of the initial observation interval together with the opponent's active continuation plan. For linear observations, recoverability of this state-plan pair is characterized by a kernel inclusion, and a bounded factorization quantifies sensitivity to observation error. In particular, the required pair may be recoverable even when the full hidden belief is not. Once initialized, the public event record and the common best-response map recursively determine subsequent opponent plans, and the resulting local algorithm reproduces an ideal benchmark on every finite opportunity prefix; implemented revisions alternate as a consequence of best-response persistence. For finite populations, we derive an eventwise linear recursion for sampling errors, obtain finite prefix error bounds, and prove record matching for an autonomous deadband rule under a positive decision margin. Finally, we separate unique solvability of mutual continuation responses from stability of alternating responses, and show that at a pre-terminal Zeno accumulation, spectral stability together with a moving-boundary estimate yields convergence of the continuation plans to the equilibrium restarted from the actual limiting state.

</details>

Abstract: In derivative-free optimization (DFO), one minimizes functions for which the gradient is unavailable or expensive to compute. In many applications, objective function values and gradients are noisy due to simulations or system randomness. This max-$M$ non-monotone condition permits temporary increases in the objective function and can help navigate narrow curved valleys; however, its theoretical analysis is significantly more challenging due to the lack of monotonic decrease. In this paper, we develop a comprehensive complexity theory for the max-$M$ non-monotone direct-search in both deterministic and stochastic DFO problems. For deterministic objectives, we establish a worst-case iteration bound for a complete poll based on a positive spanning set and an expected iteration bound for a probabilistic-descent poll. We then analyze a stochastic variant using independent function estimates and show the expected iteration complexity under tail-bound assumptions of the stochastic errors. All three results have the standard complexity of $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-2})$, which matches the iteration complexity of monotone direct-search methods. Our theory is enabled by a new family of merit functions that correct the stored objective values by ordered multiples of the squared stepsize, together with a renewal-reward stopping-time argument for the probabilistic methods.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数两人零和随机博弈具有序域性质（orderfield property），即当收益与转移概率取值于 ℝ 的某子域时，无折扣值也落在同一子域中。吸收博弈不满足该性质，Oliu-Barton 与 Vigeral 因此猜想其失效的确切范围：每个次数 m≥1 的实代数数 α 都是某个有理 m×m 吸收博弈的无折扣值。本文旨在验证这一猜想。

Method: 构造性证明：对每个给定次数 m 的实代数数 α，显式构造一个 m×m 的有理吸收博弈，使其无折扣值恰为 α。所构造的博弈位于吸收博弈的一个特殊子类中——严格吸收（strictly absorbing）且递归（recursive），即每个行动对所对应的吸收概率为正，且所有非吸收阶段的收益为零。当 α>0 时，还可进一步取为正递归（positive recursive），即吸收收益为正。

Result: 证明了该猜想成立，并给出了更强的结论：该实现博弈在严格吸收且递归的子类中即可构造；对 α>0 还可取正递归形式。作为推论，有理 m×m 吸收博弈的无折扣值的集合恰好等于次数不超过 m 的实代数数全体。

Conclusion: 吸收博弈对序域性质的失效程度被完全刻画：有理 m×m 吸收博弈的无折扣值恰为所有次数不超过 m 的实代数数，从而彻底解决 Oliu-Barton 与 Vigeral 的猜想。

Abstract: Many classes of two-player zero-sum stochastic games have the orderfield property: if all payoffs and transition probabilities lie in a subfield of $\mathbb{R}$, so does the undiscounted value. Absorbing games fail this property, and Oliu-Barton and Vigeral [\emph{Absorbing games with irrational values}, Oper.\ Res.\ Lett.\ 51 (2023) 555--559] conjectured the precise extent of the failure: every real algebraic number $α$ of degree $m\geq 1$ over $\mathbb{Q}$ is the undiscounted value of a rational $m\times m$ absorbing game. We prove this conjecture, and in fact within a special subclass of absorbing games: for every such $α$, the game realizing it is \emph{strictly absorbing} and \emph{recursive}, i.e., every action pair is absorbing with positive probability and all non-absorbing stage payoffs are zero; when $α>0$ it can moreover be taken \emph{positive recursive}, with positive absorbing payoffs. As a corollary, the set of undiscounted values of rational $m\times m$ absorbing games is exactly the set of real algebraic numbers of degree at most $m$

</details>

Abstract: This article presents a data-driven approach to minimally adjust forecast-based steady-state generation dispatch in electrical transmission networks. This result separates line loading into a dispatch-dependent baseline and an uncertainty-induced residual component. Using historical forecast errors, we then apply split conformal prediction to construct transmission-line margins with distribution-free, finite-sample linewise or joint coverage guarantees. We incorporate these calibrated quantities into a convex secure re-dispatch problem that minimally adjusts the nominal schedule while reserving sufficient transmission capacity and AGC headroom. The resulting corrected dispatch provides finite-sample probabilistic guarantees on per-line security and unsaturated AGC operation. Case studies on the RTS-GMLC system demonstrate substantial reductions in out-of-sample line-limit violations with modest operating-cost increases.

Motivation: 重元素体系存在强标量相对论效应、自旋-轨道耦合和电子关联，现有ccECP库对5d、6p区重元素覆盖不足，尤其缺乏能在精度与计算效率间取得平衡的核心-价层划分方案，制约了重元素多体量子模拟的预测能力。

Method: 发展半局域赝势与匹配基组，覆盖5d（Hf、Os、Hg）和6p（Tl、Po、At、Rn）重元素；采用分层核心-价层划分，包括60核心（Hf、Os、Hg）、78核心（主族元素）及针对Hg和Tl的中间68核心方案；以相对论全电子CCSD(T)结果为参考进行优化，并将势在原点处正则化以保证有限值以提升随机量子Monte Carlo数值稳定性。

Result: 原子低激发态全局平均偏差仅0.045 eV；分子可迁移性良好，解离能偏差低于0.03 eV，平衡键长偏差在0.005 Å以内；正则化有限原点势提升了随机量子Monte Carlo方法的数值稳定性。

Conclusion: 该扩展ccECP库为重元素体系提供了高精度、高效率的赝势与基组资源，其中68核心划分是ccECP库中的创新设计，消除了重元素多体量子模拟的关键方法学瓶颈，支持材料与分子体系的可预测性模拟。

Abstract: We expand the correlation-consistent effective core potentials (ccECPs) library by developing semi-local pseudopotentials and matching basis sets by heavy-elements from $5d$ (Hf, Os, Hg) and $6p$ (Tl, Po, At, Rn) blocks. In order to accurately capture scalar relativistic effects, spin-orbit coupling, and electron-electron correlation, we implement a tiered core-valence partitioning strategy across three distinct resolutions. This includes a small 60-core (Hf, Os, Hg) that explicitly correlates subvalence shells, a large 78-core definition for the main-group elements that rigorously accounts for core polarization and relaxation effects in sparse valence environments, and an intermediate 68-core partition for Hg and Tl. This 68-core architecture represents a unique development in the ccECP library, optimizing the balance between accuracy and computational efficiency in a manner unexplored for ligther elements. Optimized against relativistic all-electron CCSD(T) references, the ccECPs deliver outstanding atomic precision, achieving a global average atomic low-lying states deviation of just 0.045 eV. This accuracy translates directly to robust molecular transferability, systematically restricting dissociation energy discrepancies to under 0.03 eV, equilibrium bond lengths to within 0.005 Å. By enforcing a regularized, finite potential at the origin for enhanced numerical stability in stochastic quantum Monte Carlo methods, this library removes a critical methodological bottleneck for predictive many-body simulations of heavy-element systems and materials.

</details>

Result: 模型预测的电磁与热场结果与商用AISI 304不锈钢管感应焊接线的运行实测数据吻合良好；在此基础上给出了不同阻抗器材料（FeNiZnV铁氧体与Ferrotron 559H）对感应焊接过程影响的对比结果。

Conclusion: 该三维多物理场有限元框架能够准确预测工业感应焊接的电磁与热行为，具备参数化建模与物理定制网格的工程适用性，可作为阻抗器材料选型与工艺优化的有效工具。

Abstract: A three-dimensional multiphysics finite element framework for the simulation of high-frequency induction welding of tubes is presented. The model couples a time-harmonic magnetic scalar potential formulation with a stabilized quasi-stationary advection-diffusion heat transport equation, enabling accurate prediction of electromagnetic and thermal fields under industrial operating conditions. The framework incorporates parameterized geometry generation and semi-automated, physics-tailored mesh construction and is implemented using the open-source tools GetDP and Gmsh. Validation against measurements from a commercial induction welding line for AISI 304 stainless steel tubes demonstrates good agreement with operating data. The validated model is subsequently applied to investigate the influence of impeder material by comparing a conventional FeNiZnV ferrite with the soft magnetic composite Ferrotron 559H for the induction welding of AISI 304 stainless steel tubes.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次季节预报模式受粗空间分辨率限制，难以刻画极端降水；动力降尺度与深度生成降尺度均可缓解该问题，但二者在不同大气型态下对极端事件的相对性能尚不清楚。

Method: 以瑞士CombiPrecip雨量计-雷达融合观测为基准，将WRF模式与基于扩散的生成模型进行对比，对两类物理性质不同的极端降水事件开展降尺度，预见期延长至3周；为与无需针对驱动模式专门训练、可降尺度不同驱动模式边界条件的WRF公平比较，扩散模型采用非配对方式训练。

Result: 两种方法相较原始ECMWF预报均有提升，但优势依赖天气型态：WRF在多单体、非平稳事件中取得最高概率技巧；扩散模型在两类事件的不同性能指标间更一致，并在更平稳的超级单体事件中优于WRF。

Conclusion: 显式动力建模可在特定降水事件的次季节预见期上提供附加价值，而生成式降尺度则在多种不同情境下更具普适性增益。

Abstract: Coarse spatial resolution limits the ability of subseasonal prediction models to resolve extreme precipitation. Downscaling with either dynamical or deep generative models can overcome this issue, but the comparative performance of these models for extremes across different atmospheric regimes remains poorly understood. In this work, we evaluate the Weather Research and Forecasting (WRF) model against a diffusion-based generative model by downscaling two physically distinct, extreme precipitation events up to lead times of 3 weeks. For a fair comparison with WRF, which can downscale boundary conditions from different driving models without model-specific training, the diffusion model is trained in an unpaired fashion. Both approaches improve upon the raw European Centre for Medium-Range Weather Forecasts forecasts, in comparison to fused rain gauge-radar observations in Switzerland (CombiPrecip), but exhibit regime-dependent strengths. WRF achieves the highest probabilistic skill for a multicell, non-stationary event. Conversely, the diffusion model is more consistent across different performance metrics for the two events, outperforming WRF in a more stationary supercell event. These results demonstrate that explicit dynamical modeling can add value for specific precipitation events for subseasonal lead times, and that generative downscaling adds value more broadly in different situations.

</details>

### [42] [A Robust All-Mach Six-Equation Diffuse-Interface Method for Multiphase Flows with Surface Tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10727)
*Ghanshyam Bharate,J. C. Mandal*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出一个适用于宽马赫数范围的可压缩多相流有限体积框架，基于双压力六方程扩散界面模型，结合HLLC黎曼求解器、压力松弛修正与低马赫修正，实现表面张力、黏性、重力和可压缩效应的高精度模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统近似黎曼求解器在低马赫区数值耗散过大，而双压力六方程模型在含表面张力（Laplace压力跳跃）时压力松弛过程难以保持界面压差，导致界面动力学和毛细压力预测不准确，亟需兼顾宽马赫数范围稳定性和精度的数值方法。

Method: 1）采用含黏性、重力、毛细效应的双压力六方程扩散界面模型，毛细作用通过连续表面力（CSF）公式引入；2）基于广义黎曼不变量分析构造HLLC黎曼求解器，以一致处理毛细诱导的压力跳跃；3）修改瞬时压力松弛过程，使其在相平衡过程中保持Laplace压力跳跃；4）扩展此前提出的低马赫修正，引入可在强压力变化区域保持稳定的改进缩放策略，降低低马赫区的数值扩散。

Result: 在一系列涉及表面张力、黏性、重力和可压缩性的基准问题上验证方法，结果显示能准确预测界面动力学、毛细压力和低马赫流动特征，同时显著降低数值耗散且不牺牲稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提方法在从近不可压缩到可压缩的广泛流动区间内保持精度与鲁棒性，为跨流态复杂多相流模拟提供了高效可靠的途径。

Abstract: A robust finite-volume framework is presented for the simulation of compressible multiphase flows with surface tension across a wide range of Mach numbers. The method is based on a two-pressure, six-equation diffuse interface model incorporating viscous, gravitational, and capillary effects through the continuum surface force formulation. To consistently account for capillary-induced pressure jumps, an HLLC Riemann solver is developed using generalized Riemann invariant analysis, and the instantaneous pressure relaxation procedure is modified to preserve the Laplace pressure jump during phase equilibration. To overcome the excessive numerical diffusion of conventional approximate Riemann solvers in the low-Mach regime, a robust low-Mach correction is proposed by extending our previous formulation with a modified scaling strategy that remains stable in regions of strong pressure variation. The resulting method retains accuracy from nearly incompressible flows to compressible regimes while preserving the robustness of the six-equation formulation. The numerical framework is validated using a series of benchmark problems involving surface tension, viscosity, gravity, and compressibility. The results demonstrate accurate prediction of interface dynamics, capillary pressure, and low-Mach flow features, while significantly reducing numerical dissipation without compromising stability. The proposed methodology provides an efficient and reliable approach for the simulation of complex multiphase flows spanning a broad range of flow regimes.

</details>


### [43] [A Novel Multi-fidelity Surrogate for Efficient Turbine Design Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11112)
*Qineng Wang,Liming Song,Zhendong Guo,Jun Li,Zhenping Feng*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出集成加权多保真度代理模型(EMFS)及相应的MSFO优化算法,通过DBSCAN识别MFS精度不足区域并构建局部单保真度代理模型,以自适应权重融合两者引导优化,在GE-E3叶片与涡轮端壁气膜冷却布局设计中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多保真度代理模型(MFS)优化虽能通过大量廉价低保真样本辅助少量高保真样本以降低涡轮设计成本,但观测发现其有时仅在优化早期收敛更快,最终解却劣于仅用高保真样本的单保真度代理模型(SFS)。原因在于随着优化过程中高保真样本增多,低保真样本会产生负面效应,误导优化搜索。

Method: 提出集成加权多保真度代理模型(EMFS):利用基于密度的噪声应用空间聚类(DBSCAN)检测MFS无法构建更精确代理模型的区域,并在该区域构建局部SFS;随后以自适应权重将MFS与SFS组合为EMFS,用于引导优化过程。相应算法命名为多保真度与单保真度代理模型融合优化(MSFO)。

Result: 在GE-E3叶片优化以及涡轮端壁气膜冷却布局设计两项测试中,所提出的MSFO算法均得到良好验证,充分证明了其有效性。

Conclusion: MSFO通过融合多保真度与局部单保真度代理模型并采用自适应权重,有效缓解了低保真样本在优化后期误导搜索的问题,在涡轮设计优化中表现出优于单一MFS或SFS的性能。

Abstract: To solve the turbine design optimization problems efficiently, surrogate-based optimization (SBO) algorithms are frequently used. To further reduce the cost of turbine design, the multi-fidelity surrogate (MFS) based optimization is proposed by the researchers, who resort to augmenting the small number of expensive high-fidelity (HF) samples by a large portion of low-fidelity (LF) but cheap samples in surrogate modeling and optimization process. Nonetheless, according to our observations, the MFS based optimization sometimes can only have better convergence rate at the early stage of optimization process, but yielding worse final solution than the single-fidelity surrogate (SFS) based optimization that uses high-fidelity samples alone. The reason behind can be explained as follows. With the increase of HF samples in the optimization process, the LF samples can cause negative effect and therefore misleading the optimization search. To address the above issue, an ensemble weighted multi-fidelity surrogate (EMFS) is proposed. Specifically, the density-based spatial clustering of applications with noise (DBSCAN) is used to detect the region where the MFS cannot build a more accurate surrogate, and a local SFS is built there. Then, an EMFS is built by combining the MFS and SFS with adaptive weights, which is used to guide the optimization process. The related algorithm is named as multi- and single-fidelity surrogate fused optimization, i.e., MSFO. Through tests on GE-E3 blade optimization and the film cooling layout design of a turbine endwall, the effectiveness of proposed MSFO is well demonstrated.

</details>

### [44] [M3-Former: Multimodal Transformer with Mixture-of-Experts for Long-Term Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10559)
*Wenzhe Jin,Haina Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出M3-Former框架，利用LLM编码船舶静态属性与航行意图作为语义先验，结合双粒度MoE与转向加权损失，提升船舶轨迹长期预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决船舶轨迹预测中的行为多模态性、语义信息利用率低以及长期误差累积三大挑战。

Method: 构建统一多模态表示空间，用预训练LLM编码静态语义并与动态轨迹特征通过自注意力对齐；引入双粒度MoE架构，序列级专家建模全局航线规划，token级专家细化局部机动行为；设计Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy损失缓解稀疏转向样本的长尾分布问题。

Result: 在丹麦真实AIS数据集上，1至4小时预测时长内均优于现有最优基线；4小时预测任务中ADE和FDE分别降低4.4%和5.1%。

Conclusion: 语义融合有效减少长期轨迹漂移，双粒度MoE提升复杂航道与航线分叉场景的鲁棒性，建立了高层航行意图与局部运动动态联合建模的语义引导层次化预测范式。

Abstract: To address the challenges of behavioral multimodality, limited semantic utilization, and long-term error accumulation in vessel trajectory prediction, this paper proposes M3-Former, a multimodal trajectory prediction framework enhanced by large language models (LLMs). The proposed framework incorporates vessel static attributes and navigational intent as semantic priors for long-term trajectory modeling. Specifically, a unified multimodal representation space is constructed, in which static semantic information is encoded by a pre-trained LLM and aligned with dynamic trajectory features through self-attention. To jointly capture global route planning and local motion variations, a dual-granularity Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is introduced, where sequence-level experts model global navigation trends and token-level experts refine fine-grained maneuvering behaviors. In addition, a Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy loss is designed to alleviate the long-tail distribution of sparse turning samples and improve prediction accuracy in critical maneuvering scenarios. Experiments on a real-world Danish AIS dataset demonstrate that M\textsuperscript{3}-Former consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across prediction horizons from 1 to 4 hours. In the 4-hour prediction task, the proposed method reduces Average Displacement Error (ADE) and Final Displacement Error (FDE) by 4.4\% and 5.1\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline. Qualitative and ablation analyses further verify that semantic fusion effectively reduces long-term trajectory drift, while the dual-granularity MoE improves robustness in complex waterways and route-branching scenarios. The proposed framework establishes a semantic-guided hierarchical prediction paradigm, in which high-level navigational intent and local motion dynamics are jointly modeled for robust long-term vessel trajectory forecasting.

</details>


### [45] [Halo: Improving forecast accuracy through heteroscedastic estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10589)
*Adam Cataldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Halo：在深度预测器中增加一个尺度（scale）输出并用匹配的负对数似然训练，让异方差（heteroscedastic）预测在时间序列上同时提升点估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异方差预测通常只为不确定性量化服务，且在时间序列之外的异方差估计存在负面结果，因此作者希望检验并证明：在网络中额外估计尺度参数是否也能改善点预测。

Method: Halo 复用既有深度预测器架构，让其额外输出所隐含分布的尺度参数，并在高斯与拉普拉斯损失（对应负对数似然）下训练；在 transformer、图网络+变分自编码器、单层卷积网络三个 SOTA 模型上进行改造，于标准预测基准的五个电力价格市场做实验。

Result: 在 30 个“模型-市场-指标”对比中，Halo 有 28 个同时改善 MSE 与 MAE；平均 MSE 降低 2.6%–16.5%，平均 MAE 降低 1.7%–11.0%。两项发现：(1) 尺度估计来自第二个投影头还是完整并行网络影响很小，关键是否估计尺度；(2) 在已为点估计基线调好的超参数下提升仍成立，重调超参并非必需。

Conclusion: 异方差预测不仅利于不确定性量化，也能系统性提升时间序列点估计；Halo 以低成本改造即可获得稳定的 MSE/MAE 收益。

Abstract: Heteroscedastic forecasting, where a network estimates a scale parameter alongside a location parameter, is normally motivated by uncertainty quantification. This paper shows it also improves the point estimate, in contrast to reported negative results for heteroscedastic estimation outside time series. Halo is a modification that reuses an existing deep forecaster's architecture, giving it a second output for the scale of its implied distribution and training it under the matching negative log likelihood. Adapting three state-of-the-art models --- a transformer, a graph network paired with a variational autoencoder, and a single-layer convolutional network --- under both Gaussian and Laplacian losses demonstrates the phenomenon. On the five electricity price markets of a standard forecasting benchmark, Halo improves MSE and MAE in 28 of 30 model-market-metric comparisons, cutting average MSE by 2.6% to 16.5% and average MAE by 1.7% to 11.0%. Two findings emerge: (1) whether the scale estimate comes from a second projection head or from a full parallel network matters far less than whether the network estimates scale, and (2) the improvement holds under the hyperparameters already tuned for the point-estimate baseline, so retuning is optional.

</details>


### [46] [Zero-shot rib design: merging training-free generative prior with topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10643)
*Yongmin Kwon,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将冻结的文本到图像扩散模型作为免训练的设计知识来源，通过分数蒸馏采样把文本提示转化为拓扑优化中的生成梯度，与有限元灵敏度逐迭代融合，实现自然语言表达设计意图的拓扑优化，并在多域多物理情形下取得显著柔度降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然承载结构（叶脉、骨小梁、蛛网）具有高比刚度，但经典拓扑优化难以复现此类几何，且缺乏让工程师用自然语言表达设计意图的手段。作者希望借助预训练生成模型的设计先验，把文本提示作为机器可解释的设计意图表示，并让物理决定哪些生成特征得以保留。

Method: 以冻结的文本到图像扩散模型为免训练先验，采用分数蒸馏采样（SDS）将其知识蒸馏进基于密度的拓扑优化的物理循环中。每一次迭代将提示诱导的生成梯度与有限元灵敏度相结合，保留符合物理的生成特征。为抑制扩散—物理耦合造成的中间密度倾向，采用带β连续化的Heaviside投影；并构建基于骨架的自动化流程，将优化密度场转换为可供CAD使用的候选几何。

Result: 在覆盖四个几何域、两种物理机制的245次主要SDS运行中，49个提示—域组合里有38个取得统计显著的柔度降低，力学最高降低31.5%，热弹性最高降低23.0%，优于基于梯度的基线。跨域形貌分析发现反复出现的改进结构特征：多数域中生成先验抑制了肋骨架上的死端分支，端点—柔度相关系数为r=+0.56至+0.99。Heaviside投影配合β连续化将中间密度从42.6%降至3%以下。

Conclusion: 通过更换文本提示即可把生成先验迁移到不同域、载荷与物理目标（每个新问题的物理设置单独指定），该框架将预训练生成模型用作可复用、免训练的工程设计先验，使自然语言成为显式且机器可解释的设计意图表示。

Abstract: Natural load-bearing patterns such as leaf venation, trabecular bone, and spider webs achieve high stiffness per unit mass, yet classical topology optimizers rarely reach such geometries, and few let engineers express structural design intent through natural language. This work treats a frozen text-to-image diffusion model as a training-free source of design knowledge and distills it into the physics loop of density-based topology optimization via score distillation sampling, so that a text prompt becomes an explicit, machine-interpretable representation of engineer intent. The prompt-induced generative gradient and the finite element sensitivity are combined at every iteration, letting physics decide which prompt-induced features survive. In 245 primary SDS runs spanning four geometric domains and two physics regimes, 38 of 49 prompt--domain combinations achieved statistically significant compliance reductions (up to $-31.5\%$ mechanical and $-23.0\%$ thermoelastic), outperforming gradient-based baselines. Cross-domain morphological analysis identifies a recurring structural signature of improvement: in most domains the generative prior suppresses dead-end branches in the rib skeleton, with endpoint--compliance correlation $r = +0.56$ to $+0.99$. A Heaviside projection with $β$-continuation resolves a pronounced intermediate-density tendency in this diffusion--physics coupling ($42.6\%$ to $<3\%$), and an automated skeleton-based pipeline converts optimized density fields into \rev{candidate geometry ready for computer-aided design. By retargeting the generative prior across domains, loading conditions, and physics objectives through a change of text prompt, with each new problem's physics setup specified separately, the framework uses a pretrained generative model as a reusable, training-free prior for engineering design.

</details>


### [47] [Byzantine-Robust Federated Fire Detection with a Rotating Coordinator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10647)
*Georgia Argyrou,Aymen Bahrouny,Hedi Fendriy,Alexander Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将联邦学习应用于室内火灾检测，针对上行带宽受限、拜占庭客户端和固定聚合服务器单点信任三大问题，提出数据集、边缘可部署压缩检测器与半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒FL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内火灾检测依赖边缘摄像头，其敏感视频难以集中收集。现有联邦方案存在三个实际障碍：上行带宽有限、存在恶意或故障（拜占庭）客户端、以及对单一永久固定聚合服务器的无条件信任。

Method: (1) 构建由八个公开来源整理的室内火灾检测数据集；(2) 设计边缘可部署检测器，对模型更新进行最高10倍压缩；(3) 提出半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒FL方法，结合历史感知聚合与轮换协调者。

Result: 模型更新压缩最高10倍，均衡准确率仅有小幅损失；轮换协调者方法在保留测试集上与固定服务器版本在准确率和检测速度上相当，可剔除逐轮过滤漏掉的隐蔽攻击；六节点物理分布式云部署验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 所提方法同时缓解带宽、拜占庭攻击与固定服务器单点故障三个问题，并在真实分布式部署中验证了可行性与性能不退化。

Abstract: We study the application of federated learning (FL) to indoor fire detection. Such fire-detection systems use edge cameras that record sensitive footage which cannot easily be collected at a central server. Existing federated solutions leave three practical obstacles unaddressed: limited uplink bandwidth, Byzantine (malicious or faulty) clients, and unconditional trust in a single, permanently fixed aggregation server. Our main contributions address all three. In particular, we provide (i) a curated indoor fire-detection dataset assembled from eight public sources; (ii) an edge-deployable detector whose model updates are compressed up to 10 time with only a small loss in balanced accuracy; and (iii) a semi-decentralized Byzantine-robust FL method that combines history-aware aggregation with a rotating coordinator, evicting stealthy attacks that per-round filters miss while removing the fixed-server single point of failure. On the held-out test set the rotating-coordinator method matches its fixed-server counterpart in accuracy and detection speed, and a physically distributed six-node cloud deployment confirms feasibility.

</details>


### [48] [Artificial Intelligence Algorithms for the Detection of Pathologies Related to Lung Cancer through Image Analysis using Convolutional Neural Networks and Data Augmentation: a systematic mapping of the literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10652)
*Pablo Ramirez Amador*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了2015年以来AI与深度学习在肺癌影像学检测中的应用，重点为CNN结合迁移学习与数据增强，指出其诊断潜力及需解决的数据标准化、可解释性和隐私等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌是全球主要死因之一，早期诊断对改善预后至关重要；但医学影像解读复杂且依赖专业训练人员，因此需要AI与深度学习来自动化和优化影像分析流程。

Method: 在PubMed、IEEEXPLORE、Scopus和Web of Science等数据库中系统检索，筛选出2015年至今的96篇关于AI与深度学习在生物医学工程中应用的文献进行综述。

Result: 卷积神经网络结合迁移学习和数据增强被证明是提升影像解读准确性与效率的有效技术；AI与深度学习在肺癌早期诊断中表现出高敏感性和高特异性。

Conclusion: AI与深度学习可对肺癌患者诊疗产生积极影响，但其临床应用仍面临标准化数据缺乏、模型可解释性不足、患者隐私及伦理社会影响等限制，需进一步研究与规范以保障质量与可靠性。

Abstract: Lung cancer is one of the leading causes of death worldwide, and its early diagnosis is crucial to improving patients prognosis and quality of life. However, the process of interpreting medical images for the detection of lung cancer is complex and requires trained experts. In this context, artificial intelligence (AI) and deep learning (DL) emerge as potential tools to automate and optimize image analysis. The objective of this work is to review the most recent and relevant applications of AI and DL in the field of radiology for the detection of lung cancer. To this end, an exhaustive search was carried out in scientific databases such as PubMed,IEEEXPLORE, Scopus and Web of Science, and 96 articles published from 2015 to the present addressing the use of AI and DL in biomedical engineering were selected. Emphasis is placed on the use of convolutional neural networks (CNN) with transfer learning and Data Augmentation as promising techniques to improve the accuracy and efficiency of the image interpretation process. The results show that the use of AI and DL can offer an effective alternative for the early diagnosis of lung cancer, with high sensitivity and specificity. However, current limitations and challenges that must be addressed to guarantee its responsible and safe application in clinical practice are also identified, such as the lack of standardized data, the ex plainability of the models, patient privacy, and the ethical and social implications. It is concluded that the use of AI and DL can have a positive impact on the care of patients with lung cancer, but further research and regulation are required to ensure its quality and reliability.

</details>


### [49] [GEOSTEER: Geodesic Optimization for Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10658)
*Xuan Cuong Ngo,Hao Vo,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GeoSteer，一种基于优化的保范激活引导方法，将激活引导建模为黎曼流形优化问题，通过一系列小测地线步更新隐藏激活，并学习非线性目标函数自适应地指导每一步，在多个基准上优于现有激活引导基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保范激活引导方法存在两个主要局限：一是依赖预定义的引导轨迹，二是采用单步更新。这两点难以刻画激活分布的复杂结构，可能导致引导效果不稳定或不理想。因此需要一种更灵活、更贴合表示流形几何结构的方法。

Method: GeoSteer 将激活引导表述为黎曼优化问题，在表示流形上通过一系列小的测地线步（geodesic steps）迭代更新激活。为避免固定的引导方向，方法学习一个非线性激活空间目标函数，用于区分期望与不期望的激活，并据此自适应引导每一步。该多步公式在保持激活范数的同时实现平滑稳定的引导。

Result: 在 TruthfulQA、RealToxicityPrompts 和 UltraFeedback 三个基准上，GeoSteer 一致优于最先进的激活引导基线方法。

Conclusion: 用自适应、几何感知的优化替代预定义的单步编辑，可以显著提升保范激活引导的有效性，表明结合流形几何与多步优化是改进 LLM 行为控制的有前景方向。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight way to control large language models (LLMs) by modifying their hidden activations at inference time. Among these approaches, norm-preserving steering aims to change model behavior without altering the activation norm, reducing the risk of representation collapse and degradation. However, existing norm-preserving methods are limited by predefined steering trajectories and by their reliance on one-step updates, which may fail to capture the complex structure of activation distributions. We propose GeoSteer, an optimization-based method for norm-preserving activation steering. GeoSteer formulates steering as a Riemannian optimization problem and updates activations through a sequence of small geodesic steps on the representation manifold. To avoid fixed steering directions, GeoSteer learns a nonlinear activation-space objective that distinguishes desired from undesired activations, and uses this function to adaptively guide each steering step. This multistep formulation yields smoother, more stable, and more consistent steering behavior while preserving the activation norm. Across TruthfulQA, RealToxicityPrompts, and UltraFeedback benchmarks, GeoSteer consistently improves over state-of-the-art activation steering baselines. These results suggest that norm-preserving steering can be made more effective by replacing predefined one-step edits with adaptive, geometry-aware optimization.

</details>


### [50] [Conformal Calibration Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10737)
*Achref Doula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC)，在源空间有标注校准数据、目标空间仅有未标注配对观测的条件下实现共形校准迁移，并给出有限样本目标域覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测的覆盖保证依赖校准数据与部署数据的可交换性。但在实际场景中，标注校准数据只在源空间可得，而预测集需要在与源空间通过未标注配对观测（如配对模态或传感器变化）关联的目标空间使用，此时可交换性不再成立，导致覆盖失效。因此需要研究如何在无目标域标注的情况下迁移校准。

Method: 提出 TCC：利用配对数据将源空间标注校准迁移到目标空间，再仅用未标注目标输入对迁移后的残余失配进行校正。实例化两种互补校正方法：TCC-KS 使用无标签不确定性代理检测失配并保守调整校准；weighted-TCC 在权重稳定时将迁移校准向目标域重加权以提高效率。并给出适应可观测失配度量的有限样本目标域覆盖保证。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100-C、Tiny-ImageNet-C 和 SEN12MS 上，无需目标域标注校准数据即可实现可靠的目标域覆盖迁移，且无标签诊断能够预测何时需要校正。

Conclusion: TCC 能够在源-目标空间失配、仅有未标注配对数据和目标输入的情况下，实现带覆盖保证的共形校准迁移，并提供可观测失配度量下的有限样本保证与有效的无标签诊断。

Abstract: Conformal prediction converts point predictions into set-valued predictions with coverage guarantees under exchangeability between calibration and deployment data. We study conformal calibration transfer, where this requirement fails because labeled calibration is available only in a source space, while prediction sets are needed in a target space linked to the source through unlabeled paired observations (e.g., paired modalities or sensor changes). We propose Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC): we transport labeled source calibration into the target space using the paired data, and then correct residual post-transport mismatch using only unlabeled target inputs. We instantiate this correction with two complementary methods: TCC-KS, which uses a label-free uncertainty surrogate to detect mismatch and adjust calibration conservatively, and weighted-TCC, which reweights transported calibration toward the target domain for improved efficiency when weights are stable. We provide finite-sample target-domain coverage guarantees that adapt to an observable measure of mismatch. Across CIFAR-100-C, Tiny-ImageNet-C, and SEN12MS, we show reliable target-domain coverage transfer without labeled target calibration data, with label-free diagnostics that predict when correction is needed.

</details>

*Dylan Jayabahu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: A truth probe fitted where truthful reporting and a task's prescribed action coincide cannot distinguish those targets from its fitting labels alone. We call this failure of semantic identification perfect aliasing. In a controlled binary reporting game, truth and prescribed-action probes fitted on compliant contexts solve the same optimization. On rival contexts their labels are complements, forcing their AUROCs to sum to one; this identity holds across 751 cell-layer pairs to floating-point precision. We separate prescribed output symbols from semantic action using randomized codebooks, then separate truth from prescribed action by fitting on mixed compliant and rival contexts. For a reward-trained Gemma-2-9B policy that answers falsely on all evaluated rival trials, the conventional probe scores $0.006 \pm 0.005$ AUROC across three training seeds, while mixed-fit probes score $1.000$ on the same held-out activations. Mixed fitting uses more training examples and access to labelled rival contexts, so this comparison establishes linear recoverability rather than isolating the benefit of decorrelation. We also show that two compliant-fit probes, both perfect in-distribution, score $0.080$ and $0.986$ on the same rival activations. The findings concern what a probe measures: they do not establish preserved functional belief, causal use of the recovered direction, or a deployable deception detector. Code and aggregate results accompany the paper.

</details>


### [52] [Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss: Penalizing Center-Class Hedging in Ordinal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10752)
*Manisha Kandel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应边界序数损失（AMOL），通过乘性权重抑制"中心类对冲"失效模式，并引入中心对冲率（CHR）作为诊断指标，在四个序数分类基准上取得最优或并列最优QWK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失在序数分类任务中会使网络将预测向中心类偏移（即"中心类对冲"），因为预测中间类可最小化对称损失的期望，成为最省力的路径。现有序数损失虽处理大误差惩罚与排序一致性等问题，但均未针对"真实标签相对序数中心的位置"直接抑制中心类对冲。

Method: 提出自适应边界序数损失（AMOL）：对逐类损失项施加形如 m(k,y)=1+α·(1-|k-c|/c)·(|y-c|/c) 的乘性权重，其中 c 为中心类、k 为候选类、y 为真实标签。该权重编码联合条件——仅当候选类接近中心且真实标签远离中心时权重较大，否则退化为标准行为。此外引入中心对冲率（CHR）作为直接量化该失效模式的诊断指标，并提出非对称变体 AMOL-asym。

Result: 在四个序数分类基准、五个随机种子下，相较于交叉熵、OLL 和 SORD 基线，AMOL 在全部四个数据集上取得最优或并列最优的二次加权 Kappa（QWK）。非对称变体 AMOL-asym 在 Abalone 数据集上完全消除中心类对冲（五个种子下 CHR=0.000±0.000，每次运行约 n≈266 个极端类测试样本），而标准交叉熵为 0.074±0.005。

Conclusion: AMOL 通过位置敏感的乘性权重有效抑制中心类对冲，AMOL-asym 更能彻底消除该失效模式；CHR 为诊断此类失效提供了直接量化手段，验证了方法在序数分类中的有效性与针对性。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy loss causes neural networks trained on ordinal classification tasks to hedge predictions toward center classes, a failure mode we term \emph{center-class hedging}. This occurs because predicting the middle class minimizes expected symmetric loss, making it the path of least resistance regardless of the true label. Existing ordinal losses address related problems such as large-error penalization and rank consistency, but none directly suppresses center-class hedging as a function of where the true label lies relative to the ordinal center. We propose the Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss (AMOL), a multiplicative weight applied to per-class loss terms of the form $m(k,y) = 1 + α\cdot (1 - |k-c|/c) \cdot (|y-c|/c)$, where $c$ is the center class, $k$ is the candidate class, and $y$ is the true label. The weight encodes a joint condition: it is large only when the candidate class is near center and the true label is far from center, collapsing to standard behavior otherwise. We further introduce the Center-Hedging Rate (CHR) as a diagnostic metric that directly quantifies this failure mode. Across four ordinal classification benchmarks and five random seeds, AMOL achieves the best or tied-best Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) on all four datasets compared to cross-entropy, OLL, and SORD baselines. An asymmetric variant (AMOL-asym) eliminates center-class hedging entirely on the Abalone dataset ($\text{CHR} = 0.000 \pm 0.000$ across all five seeds, $n \approx 266$ extreme-class test samples per run), compared to $0.074 \pm 0.005$ for standard cross-entropy.

</details>


### [53] [A Bellman Optimality Equation for Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10776)
*Jeremy Lucas,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文初步探索在连续强化学习中优化"可塑性"（塑性-稳定性权衡的新定义），证明存在可塑性的Bellman最优方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续强化学习中稳定性-可塑性权衡是核心难题；Abel等(2025)将其重新表述为"赋权-可塑性"权衡，但缺乏对可塑性优化的研究。

Method: 在马尔可夫决策过程框架下，针对可塑性（观测到动作的广义有向信息）构造优化目标。

Result: 证明存在与赋权优化类似的可塑性Bellman最优方程。

Conclusion: 为在新定义下优化可塑性提供了初步理论基础。
